# Provider app and admin dashboard: every screen, every element, what it is wired to

Generated by `node tools/qa/screens-report.mjs` from the static inventories (`docs/design/inventory/*`, written by `tools/design/screen-inventory.mjs`). Do not edit by hand.

**Status:** OK = wired to a real backend endpoint (route and method checked against the controllers), local logic, navigation or a parent callback, nothing suspicious · FIXED = was flagged and fixed (`docs/qa/provider-screens-fixed.json`) · MISSING-BACKEND = calls an endpoint the backend does not have · FLAGGED = dead handler, mock data or placeholder, not yet resolved.

**Limits (stated, not hidden):** static and heuristic analysis. A prop-fed list or a handler passed down from a parent is traced only inside its own component. Response field names and per-field validation (required, format, min/max, the error text) are not compared by this report; `tools/audit/dtocheck.js` checks that every field a client sends exists in the backend DTO. A control can be wired to a real endpoint and still behave wrongly: that needs the live runs in `docs/qa/provider-journeys.md`.
